<a id="pm-s1"></a>
## 1. Abstract and result contract

> We prove the complex group-ring Bass trace conjecture for every discrete group: the Hattori–Stallings trace of a finitely generated projective module over its complex group ring is supported on conjugacy classes of finite-order elements. As a consequence, for every torsion-free group <i>G</i> and every commutative unital domain <i>R</i> of characteristic zero, the only idempotents in $`RG`$ are 0 and 1. This proves Kaplansky's idempotent conjecture in characteristic zero.

- **C1** (theorem, [source](#thm:bass)): Let $G$ be any discrete group. For every $x\in K_0(\mathbb CG)$ and every infinite-order element $g\in G$, $$\mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_G(x)([g]_G)=0.$$ Equivalently, if $\mathop{\mathrm{con}}_{\mathrm{fin}}(G)$ denotes the conjugacy classes of finite-order elements, then $$\mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_G\bigl(K_0(\mathbb CG)\bigr) \subseteq\bigoplus_{C\in\mathop{\mathrm{con}}_{\mathrm{fin}}(G)}\mathbb CC.$$
  - Explanation: The paper asserts vanishing of projective trace coefficients on every infinite-order conjugacy class, while permitting finite-order support.
- **C2** (theorem, [source](#cor:torsion-free)): Let $G$ be any torsion-free discrete group.* 1. *For every $q\geq1$ and every idempotent $e\in M_q(\mathbb CG)$, $$\tau_{G,q}(e)=\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb C}\bigl(\epsilon_{G,q}(e)\bigr).$$ Consequently $\tau_{G,*}=\operatorname{rk}_{\epsilon}$, with $\mathbb Z$ viewed as a subgroup of $\mathbb C$, and $$\tau_{G,*}\bigl(K_0(\mathbb CG)\bigr)=\mathbb Z.$$* 2. *For every commutative unital domain $R$ of characteristic zero, every $e\in RG$ satisfying $e^2=e$ is either $0$ or $1$.
  - Explanation: For torsion-free groups, matrix trace equals augmentation rank. Scalar idempotents over a commutative characteristic-zero domain are trivial.
- **C3** (theorem, [source](#thm:sparse)): For each integer $D\geq2$ there is an integer $n_0(D)$ with the following property. Let $Y$ be the vertex set of a countable connected simple graph of degree at most $D$, and let a countable group $H$ act by graph automorphisms, freely on vertices. Suppose $n\geq n_0(D)$ and $K$ is an $H$-invariant subcomplex of the full simplex on $Y$ such that:* 1. *$K$ is generated by its $n$-simplices and has finitely many $H$-orbits of simplices;* 2. *the vertices of each $n$-simplex span a connected subgraph of $Y$.* *Then every $H$-invariant cocycle $\psi:E_n\to\mathbb C$ evaluates to zero on the image in $(E_n)_H$ of every cycle in $C_n(K)_H$.
  - Explanation: Connected supports in a fixed bounded-degree graph force invariant cocycle evaluations on coinvariant cycles to vanish in sufficiently high dimension.
- **C4** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:proper)): Every closed $\rho$-ball meets only finitely many simplices of $K$. The complex $K$ is locally finite, and $\rho$ gives its usual realization topology and is proper. For each $c\in\|K\|$, $s\geq0$, and simplex $\kappa$, the set $$\{h\in H:L_s(hc)\cap\kappa\ne\varnothing\}$$ is finite. The setwise stabilizer $H_\kappa$ of a nonempty simplex is finite as well.
  - Explanation: The transportation metric has finite local geometry and finite simplex stabilizers under the stated free graph action.
- **C5** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:tree)): In every tree Euclidean structure on a top simplex $\sigma$, its $n$-volume is $1/n!$. Every affine branch of $f_c\|_\sigma$ has gradient of norm at most one, for every $c\in\|K\|$. The same gradient bound holds after restriction to any affine subspace of the span of $\sigma$.
  - Explanation: Tree-edge coordinates give the factorial simplex volume and control all distance gradients.
- **C6** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:free-parameter)): Every countable group $H$ admits a continuous free action on a compact metrizable zero-dimensional space $X$, preserving a Borel probability measure $\mu$.
  - Explanation: A compact zero-dimensional probability parameter separates translates without an amenability assumption.
- **C7** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:refinement)): Fix locally constant equivariant triangulation families $T$ and $S$, where $S$ refines $T$, and subcomplex families $P\subseteq T$ and $Z\subseteq S$ with $Z\subseteq P$. Let $\{(c_\alpha,B_\alpha)\}_{\alpha\in A}$ be a countable equivariant family of centers $c_\alpha\in\|K\|$ and clopen sets $B_\alpha\subseteq X$. Assume that only finitely many of the balls $L_R(c_\alpha)$ meet any given original simplex. Set $$\mathcal U_t(x)=\bigcup_{\alpha:\,x\in B_\alpha}U_t(c_\alpha), \qquad \mathcal S_t(x)=\bigcup_{\alpha:\,x\in B_\alpha}S_t(c_\alpha).$$ Outside a countable set of radii $t\in(r,R)$, there is a locally constant equivariant triangulation refining $S$ in which both $$\label{eq:cut-replacement} \begin{gathered} P(x)\cap\mathcal S_t(x),\\ \bigl(Z(x)\setminus\mathcal U_t(x)\bigr) \cup\bigl(P(x)\cap\mathcal S_t(x)\bigr) \end{gathered}$$ are subcomplexes. Each inserted level meets every face $F$ of $T$ or $S$ in dimension at most $\dim F-1$. Consequently, if $Z$ satisfies the dimension-drop condition relative to $T$, both displayed sets do also. If every component of $P\setminus Z$ has diameter at most $2R$, the second displayed set preserves that property.
  - Explanation: Generic cuts admit compatible equivariant refinements while preserving dimension drop and bounded component diameter.
- **C8** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:filtration-exists)): For any allowed choices through stage $d$, an allowed choice of $(T_{d-1},Z_{d-1})$ exists. Hence a filtration can be continued to $Z_0$.
  - Explanation: An admissible separating filtration can be continued through every lower stratum.
- **C9** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:carrier)): Let $(T_i,Z_i)_{i=0}^n$ be an allowed filtration and let $z\in Z_0(x)$. For every $i$, there is a unique $i$-simplex of $T_i(x)$ contained in $Z_i(x)$ and containing $z$. The point $z$ lies in its relative interior. In particular, every point of $Z_0(x)$ lies in the interior of an original top simplex.
  - Explanation: Zero-stratum points have unique interior carrier simplices at every filtration stage.
- **C10** (conditional_result, [source](#prop:zero-strata)): For every $\epsilon>0$ there is an admissible equivariant locally constant filtration $(Z_j,T_j)_{j=0}^n$ such that, for every original top simplex $\sigma$ of $K$, $$\label{eq:zero-strata} \int_X\#\bigl(Z_0(x)\cap\sigma\bigr)\,d\mu(x) \le C_\sigma\epsilon.$$ Here $C_\sigma$ depends on the fixed geometric data but is independent of $\epsilon$ and of the complexity of the chosen triangulations.
  - Explanation: Near-minimizing separators give an arbitrarily small expected zero-stratum count in each original top simplex.
- **C11** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:finite-component-labels)): For every original simplex $\kappa$ of $K$, there is a finite set $W_\kappa\subseteq Y$, independent of $\epsilon$ and $x$, such that $J_{U_F}\subseteq W_\kappa$ whenever $b_F\in\kappa$. The triangulation over $\kappa$, together with all its assigned vectors $u_{U_F}$, is locally constant in $x$. These assignments are equivariant.
  - Explanation: Component labels remain inside a fixed finite vertex set near each original simplex and vary locally constantly with the parameter.
- **C12** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:surviving-flags)): For every original top simplex $\sigma$ and every $x$, at most $$2^n\#\bigl(Z_0(x)\cap\sigma\bigr)$$ terms in its fundamental barycentric subdivision chain have nonzero images under the exterior replacement map.
  - Explanation: Carrier geometry bounds surviving subdivision flags by the zero-stratum point count.
- **C13** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:equivariant-homotopy)): Let $K$ be a simplicial complex with an $H$-action and finite setwise simplex stabilizers. Let $E_*$ be the augmented exterior chain complex on a nonempty $H$-set. Any two $H$-equivariant augmented chain maps $A,B:C_*(K)\to E_*$ that agree in degree $-1$ are $H$-equivariantly chain homotopic.
  - Explanation: Finite stabilizer averaging turns nonequivariant fillers into an equivariant chain homotopy.
- **C14** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:locality)): Let $i:L\hookrightarrow G$ be an inclusion of discrete groups. Extension of scalars defines $i_*:K_0(\mathbb CL)\to K_0(\mathbb CG)$, and for every $y\in K_0(\mathbb CL)$ and $C\in\mathop{\mathrm{con}}(G)$, $$\label{eq:class-aggregation} \mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_G(i_*y)(C) =\sum_{\substack{B\in\mathop{\mathrm{con}}(L)\\ B\subseteq L\cap C}} \mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_L(y)(B).$$ Only finitely many terms are nonzero. Moreover, every $x\in K_0(\mathbb CG)$ is induced from a finitely generated subgroup, which may be required to contain any prescribed finite subset of $G$. Consequently, the finite-order-support assertion of Theorem <a href="#thm:bass" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="thm:bass">1</a> for all finitely generated groups implies that assertion for all discrete groups.
  - Explanation: Finite coefficient support reduces arbitrary groups to finitely generated subgroups, with exact aggregation over subgroup conjugacy classes.
- **C15** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:path-identities)): For the path sums associated to $e$ and $g$, the twisted cyclic shift $$T(h_0,\ldots,h_{k-1})=(h_1,\ldots,h_{k-1},gh_0)$$ preserves $v_k$. For $k\ge2$, deletion of any entry induces a map $d_i:Q_k\to Q_{k-1}$ satisfying $$(d_i)_*v_k=v_{k-1}\qquad(0\le i<k).$$ Every $v_k$ has the same total weight $$\label{eq:trace-coefficient} \lambda:=v_k(1) =\sum_{s\in[g]_G}\mathop{\mathrm{tr}}(A_s) =\mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_G\bigl([e(\mathbb CG)^r]\bigr)([g]_G).$$
  - Explanation: Idempotence yields deletion identities and matrix trace cyclicity yields twisted-shift invariance; their common total weight is the target trace coefficient.
- **C16** (conditional_result, [source](#prop:path-cycle)): For every positive even integer $n$, the chain $z_n$ is a cycle. Moreover, either its defining sum has no nonzero distinct-vertex terms, so $z_n=0$, or it is the image of a cycle in $C_n(K)_H$ for an $H$-invariant simplicial complex $K$ generated by $n$-simplices, with finitely many simplex orbits, whose top vertex sets span connected subgraphs of $Y$. The graph and its degree bound $D$ are independent of $n$.
  - Explanation: Even-dimensional exterior path chains are coinvariant cycles supported on connected vertex sets in a fixed graph.
- **C17** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:beta)): There is a $J$-invariant function $\beta:G\times G\to\mathbb R$ such that $$\label{eq:beta-properties} \beta(h',h)=-\beta(h,h'),\qquad \beta(g^r h,g^s h')=\beta(h,h')+s-r \quad(r,s\in\mathbb Z).$$
  - Explanation: An antisymmetric exponent-offset function is invariant under the centralizer and changes predictably when lifts are shifted.
- **C18** (conditional_result, [source](#lem:pfaffian-recurrence)): For every $m\ge1$, one has $q_m=\tfrac12q_{m-1}$ and hence $q_m=2^{-m}\lambda$.
  - Explanation: The twisted Pfaffian rotation and deletion identities give a halving recurrence for weighted Pfaffian sums.
- **C19** (conditional_result, [source](#prop:detector)): For every integer $m\ge1$, the formula $$\label{eq:detector-formula} \psi_m(y_0,\ldots,y_{2m}) =\sum_{i=0}^{2m}(-1)^i p_m(h_0,\ldots,\widehat{h_i},\ldots,h_{2m}),$$ where $h_i\in G$ is any lift of $y_i\in Y$, defines an $H$-invariant linear functional $\psi_m:E_{2m}\to\mathbb C$ satisfying $$\psi_m\partial=0,\qquad \psi_m(z_{2m})=2^{-m}\lambda.$$ Here evaluation on the coinvariant chain $z_{2m}$ is defined by $H$-invariance.
  - Explanation: The alternating Pfaffian deletion sum descends to an invariant exterior cocycle and detects the trace coefficient.
- **C20** (theorem, [source](#cor:integral)): For every discrete group $G$, the Hattori–Stallings trace of every class in $K_0(\mathbb ZG)$ is supported on $[1]_G$.
  - Explanation: The integral trace is supported on the identity by combining complex trace vanishing with the cited restriction on finite-order coefficients.
- **C21** (theorem, [source](#cor:homotopy-idempotents)): Let $M$ be a connected closed smooth oriented manifold.* 1. *If $\dim M>2$ and $f:M\to M$ satisfies $f\circ f\simeq f$ by free homotopy, then some self-map freely homotopic to $f$ has exactly one fixed point.* 2. *Choose $x_0\in M$ and put $G=\pi_1(M,x_0)$. If $f:(M,x_0)\to(M,x_0)$ satisfies $f\circ f\simeq_* f$ by based homotopy and $f_\#=\mathop{\mathrm{id}}_G$, then $$L(f)=L^{(2)}(\widetilde f).$$ Here $\widetilde f$ is the lift to the universal cover fixing a chosen point above $x_0$; it is $G$-equivariant. The left side is the ordinary Lefschetz number, and the right side is the alternating sum of von Neumann traces on the induced $L^2$-homology maps, as in (Berrick, Chatterji, and Mislin 2007, Definition 7.1) [[BCM2006](#ref-BCM2006)].
  - Explanation: Integral trace support yields the stated fixed-point and Lefschetz consequences under their separate manifold and homotopy hypotheses.

Let $\\{(c_\\alpha,B_\\alpha)\\}_{\\alpha\\in A}$ be a countable equivariant family of centers $c_\\alpha\\in|K|$ and clopen sets $B_\\alpha\\subseteq X$. Assume that only finitely many of the balls $L_R(c_\\alpha)$ meet any given original simplex. Set $$\\mathcal U_t(x)=\\bigcup_{\\alpha:\\,x\\in B_\\alpha}U_t(c_\\alpha),\n \\qquad\n \\mathcal S_t(x)=\\bigcup_{\\alpha:\\,x\\in B_\\alpha}S_t(c_\\alpha).$$ Outside a countable set of radii $t\\in(r,R)$, there is a locally constant equivariant triangulation refining $S$ in which both $$\\label{eq:cut-replacement}\n\\begin{gathered}\n P(x)\\cap\\mathcal S_t(x),\\\\\n \\bigl(Z(x)\\setminus\\mathcal U_t(x)\\bigr)\n       \\cup\\bigl(P(x)\\cap\\mathcal S_t(x)\\bigr)\n\\end{gathered}$$ are subcomplexes. Each inserted level meets every face $F$ of $T$ or $S$ in dimension at most $\\dim F-1$. Consequently, if $Z$ satisfies the dimension-drop condition relative to $T$, both displayed sets do also. If every component of $P\\setminus Z$ has diameter at most $2R$, the second displayed set preserves that property.",
```json
          "source_id": "canonical:PM-207.2"
        }
      ],
      "statement_anchor": "lem:refinement"
    },

        "anchor": "lem:locality",
        "exact_quote": "Let $i:L\\hookrightarrow G$ be an inclusion of discrete groups. Extension of scalars defines $i_*:K_0(\\mathbb CL)\\to K_0(\\mathbb CG)$, and for every $y\\in K_0(\\mathbb CL)$ and $C\\in\\mathop{\\mathrm{con}}(G)$, $$\\label{eq:class-aggregation}\n \\mathop{\\mathrm{HS}}_G(i_*y)(C)\n =\\sum_{\\substack{B\\in\\mathop{\\mathrm{con}}(L)\\\\ B\\subseteq L\\cap C}}\n       \\mathop{\\mathrm{HS}}_L(y)(B).$$ Only finitely many terms are nonzero. Moreover, every $x\\in K_0(\\mathbb CG)$ is induced from a finitely generated subgroup, which may be required to contain any prescribed finite subset of $G$. Consequently, the finite-order-support assertion of Theorem <a href=\"#thm:bass\" data-reference-type=\"ref\" data-reference=\"thm:bass\">1</a> for all finitely generated groups implies that assertion for all discrete groups.",

        "exact_quote": "For every positive even integer $n$, the chain $z_n$ is a cycle.         "exact_quote": "Let $M$ be a connected closed smooth oriented manifold.*\n\n1.  *If $\\dim M>2$ and $f:M\\to M$ satisfies $f\\circ f\\simeq f$ by free homotopy, then some self-map freely homotopic to $f$ has exactly one fixed point.*\n\n2.  *Choose $x_0\\in M$ and put $G=\\pi_1(M,x_0)$. If $f:(M,x_0)\\to(M,x_0)$ satisfies $f\\circ f\\simeq_* f$ by based homotopy and $f_\\#=\\mathop{\\mathrm{id}}_G$, then $$L(f)=L^{(2)}(\\widetilde f).$$ Here $\\widetilde f$ is the lift to the universal cover fixing a chosen point above $x_0$; it is $G$-equivariant. The left side is the ordinary Lefschetz number, and the right side is the alternating sum of von Neumann traces on the induced $L^2$-homology maps, as in (Berrick, Chatterji, and Mislin 2007, Definition 7.1) [[BCM2006](#ref-BCM2006)].",
```

<a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **Grothendieck group and zeroth Hochschild quotient** ([source](#sec:introduction)): For a unital complex algebra $A$, let $K_0(A)$ be the Grothendieck group of finitely generated projective right $A$-modules, and set $$\mathop{\mathrm{HH}}_0(A)=A/[A,A],$$ where $[A,A]$ is the complex linear span of commutators.
- **Hattori–Stallings trace** ([source](#sec:introduction)): Its Hattori–Stallings trace is $$\mathop{\mathrm{HS}}_A([eA^q])=\left[\sum_{i=1}^q e_{ii}\right]\in\mathop{\mathrm{HH}}_0(A),$$ extended additively to virtual projective classes.
- **Canonical trace and augmentation** ([source](#pm-h-3)): Define the canonical trace and the augmentation by $$\tau_G\!\left(\sum_{g\in G}a_g g\right)=a_1,\qquad \epsilon_G\!\left(\sum_{g\in G}a_g g\right)=\sum_{g\in G}a_g.$$
- **Augmentation rank** ([source](#pm-h-3)): The augmentation also defines the integer-valued augmentation rank $$\operatorname{rk}_{\epsilon}:K_0(\mathbb CG) \xrightarrow{(\epsilon_G)_*}K_0(\mathbb C) \xrightarrow{\dim_{\mathbb C}}\mathbb Z.$$
- **Augmented exterior chains** ([source](#sec:framework)): The augmented chains of the full simplex on $Y$ are $$E_j=\bigwedge^{j+1}\mathbb C^{(Y)}\quad(j\geq0),\qquad E_{-1}=\mathbb C.$$
- **Coinvariants** ([source](#sec:framework)): For an $H$-module $V$, its coinvariants are $$V_H=V/\operatorname{span}_{\mathbb C}\{hv-v:h\in H,\ v\in V\}.$$
- **Scaled transportation metric** ([source](#eq:transport-metric)): Represent a point of $\|K\|$ by a probability vector $p$ on $Y$ whose support lies in a simplex of $K$. Let $d_Y$ denote graph distance and define $$\rho(p,q)=\frac1{\sqrt n} \sup_{\operatorname{Lip}_{d_Y}(\ell)\leq1} \sum_{v\in Y}\ell(v)\bigl(p(v)-q(v)\bigr). \label{eq:transport-metric}$$
- **Component support and average** ([source](#pm-h-20)): For any such component $U$, set $$\label{eq:component-average} J_U=\bigcup_{p\in U}\mathop{\mathrm{supp}}p, \qquad u_U=\frac{1}{\|J_U\|}\sum_{v\in J_U}\delta_v.$$
- **Averaged chain map** ([source](#pm-h-20)): We may therefore define $$\label{eq:averaged-chain-map} F^\epsilon(c)=\int_X f_x(c)\,d\mu(x).$$
- **Path weight** ([source](#eq:path-weight)): Define the weight of a tuple by $$\label{eq:path-weight} w_k(h_0,\ldots,h_{k-1}) =\mathop{\mathrm{tr}}\bigl(a(h_0,h_1)\cdots a(h_{k-2},h_{k-1})a(h_{k-1},gh_0)\bigr),$$ where $w_1(h_0)=\mathop{\mathrm{tr}}(a(h_0,gh_0))$.
- **Weighted Pfaffian sums** ([source](#pm-h-28)): The finite weighted sums $$q_m=v_{2m}(p_m)\quad(m\ge1),\qquad q_0=\lambda$$ are therefore defined.

```json
      "exact_definition": "Define the weight of a tuple by $$\\label{eq:path-weight}\n w_k(h_0,\\ldots,h_{k-1})\n =\\mathop{\\mathrm{tr}}\\bigl(a(h_0,h_1)\\cdots\n a(h_{k-2},h_{k-1})a(h_{k-1},gh_0)\\bigr),$$ where $w_1(h_0)=\\mathop{\\mathrm{tr}}(a(h_0,gh_0))$.",
      "interpretation": "Ordered kernel products are closed by the twisted endpoint before taking matrix trace.",
      "kind": "definition",
      "name": "Path weight",

    },
```

## 3. Proof roadmap

- **R1** [claim](#lem:tree) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-tree): Tree-edge coordinates and Lipschitz gradient control -> Factorial volumes and controlled gradients
- **R2** [claim](#prop:zero-strata) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-zero-strata): Successive near minimizers, disjoint parameter-space replacements, and iterated slicing -> Arbitrarily small expected zero-stratum counts
- **R3** [claim](#lem:surviving-flags) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-surviving-flags): Component averaging and carrier flag counting -> Small invariant cocycle evaluations
- **R4** [claim](#thm:sparse) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-equivariant-homotopy-2): Finite stabilizer averaging and equivariant homotopy before taking coinvariants -> Sparse-chain vanishing
- **R5** [claim](#prop:path-cycle) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-path-cycle): Kernel idempotence and twisted cyclic shift -> Even-dimensional connected-support cycles
- **R6** [claim](#prop:detector) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-detector): Antisymmetric lift offsets, Pfaffian rotation, and alternating deletion -> Invariant cocycle detecting the trace coefficient
- **R7** [claim](#thm:bass) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-detector-2): Fix the graph before choosing a sufficiently large even dimension, then aggregate subgroup coefficients -> Vanishing on infinite-order conjugacy classes
- **R8** [claim](#cor:integral) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-integral): Coefficientwise scalar extension and the cited finite-order restriction -> Identity support of integral traces
- **R9** [claim](#cor:torsion-free) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-homotopy-idempotents-3): Augmentation rank, positive similarity, faithful trace, and finite-coefficient field embedding -> Trace-rank equality and scalar idempotent triviality
- **R10** [claim](#cor:homotopy-idempotents) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-homotopy-idempotents): Transport module conventions and apply the cited fixed-point equivalences -> The stated fixed-point and Lefschetz consequences

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **I1**: The algebraic complex group-ring trace vanishes at infinite-order conjugacy classes.
- **I2**: The alternating Pfaffian deletion expression descends to an invariant exterior cocycle and detects the scaled trace coefficient.
- **I3**: Sparse-chain vanishing requires sufficiently high dimension depending on the fixed graph degree.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **A1**: Conclude that all idempotent matrices are trivial. -> Matrix augmentation rank gives a numerical conclusion while nontrivial matrix idempotents remain.
- **A2**: Extend the integer trace range to every class in the reduced completion. -> The comparison controls only the algebraic image.
- **A3**: Apply the coefficient reduction in positive characteristic. -> The field-embedding argument has no stated positive-characteristic extension.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `constructive`. The Pfaffian deletion formula is a finite algebraic construction on a supplied list of lifts. This implementation checks its rational matrix expression; it does not construct the group action or certify the trace theorem.

```python
# INPUTS: JSON list of odd square skew-symmetric rational matrices of orders 1, 3, or 5.
# PRECONDITIONS: Entries are integer or rational strings; diagonals vanish; batch size at most 64.
# OUTPUTS: JSON list of exact rational alternating deletion sums of Pfaffians.
# TIME: O(B N (N-1)!); rational operation count, excluding integer bit costs.
# SPACE: O(B N^2 + N^3); excluding exact integer bit storage.
# ARITHMETIC: Exact python-flint fmpq arithmetic; no floating point.
# FAILURE: Malformed or out-of-contract input raises an exception; no approximate result.
import json, sys
from flint import fmpq

def pf(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return fmpq(1)
    total = fmpq(0)
    for j in range(1, n):
        keep = [k for k in range(n) if k != 0 and k != j]
        sub = [[a[u][v] for v in keep] for u in keep]
        total += (-1)**(j+1) * a[0][j] * pf(sub)
    return total

def deletion(a):
    total = fmpq(0)
    for i in range(len(a)):
        keep = [k for k in range(len(a)) if k != i]
        minor = [[a[u][v] for v in keep] for u in keep]
        total += (-1)**i * pf(minor)
    return str(total)

raw = json.loads(sys.stdin.read())
assert isinstance(raw, list) and 1 <= len(raw) <= 64
answer = []
for rows in raw:
    n = len(rows)
    assert n in (1, 3, 5) and all(len(row) == n for row in rows)
    a = [[fmpq(str(x)) for x in row] for row in rows]
    assert all(a[i][i] == 0 for i in range(n))
    assert all(a[i][j] == -a[j][i] for i in range(n) for j in range(n))
    answer.append(deletion(a))
print(json.dumps(answer))
```

```json
          }
        ],
        "id": "I3",
        "quantifiers": [
          {
            "anchor": "stmt-thm-sparse",
            "exact_quote": "For each integer $D\\geq2$ there is an integer $n_0(D)$ with the following property. Let $Y$ be the vertex set of a countable connected simple graph of degree at most $D$, and let a countable group $H$ act by graph automorphisms, freely on vertices. Suppose $n\\geq n_0(D)$ and $K$ is an $H$-invariant subcomplex of the full simplex on $Y$ such that:*\n\n1.  *$K$ is generated by its $n$-simplices and has finitely many $H$-orbits of simplices;*\n\n2.  *the vertices of each $n$-simplex span a connected subgraph of $Y$.*\n\n*Then every $H$-invariant cocycle $\\psi:E_n\\to\\mathbb C$ evaluates to zero on the image in $(E_n)_H$ of every cycle in $C_n(K)_H$.",
            "source_object": "sparse-chain hypotheses and threshold"
          }
        ],
        "regime": "Countable bounded-degree graphs with free vertex actions and connected top supports.",
        "statement": "Sparse-chain vanishing requires sufficiently high dimension depending on the fixed graph degree.",
        "verification": "PENDING"
      }

    "input_contract": "JSON batch of at most 64 odd skew-symmetric matrices, orders 1, 3 or 5, with exact rational entries. Order 1 is the empty-Pfaffian boundary; orders 3 and 5 represent the detector expression.",

    "oracle_code": "import sys as system\nimport json as codec\nfrom fractions import Fraction as Q\nfrom itertools import permutations\nfrom math import factorial\n\ndef alternating_pfaffian(matrix):\n    size = len(matrix)\n    accumulation = Q(0)\n    for order in permutations(range(size)):\n        inversions = sum(order[x] > order[y] for x in range(size) for y in range(x+1, size))\n        term = Q(-1 if inversions % 2 else 1)\n        for pair in range(0, size, 2):\n            term *= matrix[order[pair]][order[pair+1]]\n        accumulation += term\n    return accumulation / (2**(size//2) * factorial(size//2))\n\ndef expression(matrix):\n    result = Q(0)\n    for omitted in range(len(matrix)):\n        face = [row[:omitted] + row[omitted+1:] for pos,row in enumerate(matrix) if pos != omitted]\n        result += (1 if omitted % 2 == 0 else -1) * alternating_pfaffian(face)\n    return result\n\ntry:\n    payload = codec.loads(open(system.argv[1]).read())\n    calculated = []\n    for item in payload:\n        matrix = [[Q(entry) for entry in row] for row in item]\n        value = expression(matrix)\n        offsets = [Q(pos-1, 2) for pos in range(len(matrix))]\n        changed = [[entry + offsets[col] - offsets[row] for col,entry in enumerate(line)] for row,line in enumerate(matrix)]\n        assert expression(changed) == value\n        calculated.append(str(value))\n    expected = codec.loads(system.argv[3])\n    observed = codec.loads(open(system.argv[2]).read())\n    system.exit(0 if observed == calculated == expected else 7)\nexcept Exception:\n    system.exit(9)\n",
    "output_contract": "Exact rational values of the alternating Pfaffian deletion expression.",
    "reason": "The Pfaffian deletion formula is a finite algebraic construction on a supplied list of lifts. This implementation checks its rational matrix expression; it does not construct the group action or certify the trace theorem.",
    "reference_code": "# INPUTS: JSON list of odd square skew-symmetric rational matrices of orders 1, 3, or 5.\n# PRECONDITIONS: Entries are integer or rational strings; diagonals vanish; batch size at most 64.\n# OUTPUTS: JSON list of exact rational alternating deletion sums of Pfaffians.\n# TIME: O(B N (N-1)!); rational operation count, excluding integer bit costs.\n# SPACE: O(B N^2 + N^3); excluding exact integer bit storage.\n# ARITHMETIC: Exact python-flint fmpq arithmetic; no floating point.\n# FAILURE: Malformed or out-of-contract input raises an exception; no approximate result.\nimport json, sys\nfrom flint import fmpq\n\ndef pf(a):\n    n = len(a)\n    if n == 0:\n        return fmpq(1)\n    total = fmpq(0)\n    for j in range(1, n):\n        keep = [k for k in range(n) if k != 0 and k != j]\n        sub = [[a[u][v] for v in keep] for u in keep]\n        total += (-1)**(j+1) * a[0][j] * pf(sub)\n    return total\n\ndef deletion(a):\n    total = fmpq(0)\n    for i in range(len(a)):\n        keep = [k for k in range(len(a)) if k != i]\n        minor = [[a[u][v] for v in keep] for u in keep]\n        total += (-1)**i * pf(minor)\n    return str(total)\n\nraw = json.loads(sys.stdin.read())\nassert isinstance(raw, list) and 1 <= len(raw) <= 64\nanswer = []\nfor rows in raw:\n    n = len(rows)\n    assert n in (1, 3, 5) and all(len(row) == n for row in rows)\n    a = [[fmpq(str(x)) for x in row] for row in rows]\n    assert all(a[i][i] == 0 for i in range(n))\n    assert all(a[i][j] == -a[j][i] for i in range(n) for j in range(n))\n    answer.append(deletion(a))\nprint(json.dumps(answer))\n",
```

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **L1** (counterexample_scope): Finite-order trace coefficients in the complex group ring can occur away from the identity. Blocked use: ['Identity-only support for complex group rings with torsion.']
- **L2** (hypothesis): The scalar idempotent conclusion requires a torsion-free group and a commutative unital coefficient domain of characteristic zero. Blocked use: ['Applying scalar idempotent triviality after dropping torsion-freeness or the coefficient-domain hypotheses.']
- **L3** (edge_case): Matrix idempotents need not be zero or the identity; their trace conclusion is numerical. Blocked use: ['Using scalar idempotent triviality for arbitrary idempotent matrices.']
- **L4** (formal_scope): The integer trace range in the reduced completion is established on the algebraic image only. Blocked use: ['Asserting the integer trace range for every topological class in the reduced completion.']
- **L5** (formal_scope): The argument does not establish an extension of algebraic augmentation to the reduced completion. Blocked use: ['Evaluating algebraic augmentation on arbitrary elements of the reduced completion.']
- **L6** (unknown): The reverse implication from the idempotent conjecture to the zero-divisor conjecture is not established here. Blocked use: ['Treating scalar idempotent triviality as a proof that the group ring has no zero divisors.']
- **L7** (hypothesis): Sparse-chain vanishing requires sufficiently high dimension for a fixed graph degree, a free graph action, finitely many simplex orbits, and connected top-simplex supports. Blocked use: ['Applying vanishing to arbitrary low-dimensional cycles or unsupported group actions.']
- **L8** (edge_case): The cocycle need not be globally bounded; the evaluation estimate uses a finite set attached to each original simplex. Blocked use: ['Replacing local finite support control with an unproved global cocycle bound.']
- **L9** (nonconstructive): Separators are chosen near an infimum by existence; the argument uses neither an attained minimum nor a limit of filtrations. Blocked use: ['Claiming an executable separator optimiser or a runtime bound from the existence proof.']
- **L10** (hypothesis): The strict volume margin is obtained with graph degree fixed as dimension increases. Blocked use: ['Increasing graph degree with dimension while retaining the same asymptotic margin.']
- **L11** (edge_case): A finite representative of a coinvariant cycle need not be a cycle before taking coinvariants. Blocked use: ['Checking only ordinary boundaries when the claimed result concerns coinvariant cycles.']
- **L12** (hypothesis): The trace construction uses finite group-ring coefficient support, without a claim about completed group algebras. Blocked use: ['Extending finite path-sum constructions to arbitrary completed-algebra elements.']
- **L13** (hypothesis): The single-fixed-point consequence requires a connected closed smooth oriented manifold with dimension greater than two and a free homotopy idempotent. Blocked use: ['Removing the manifold, dimension, or homotopy hypotheses from the fixed-point consequence.']
- **L14** (formal_scope): A finite Pfaffian deletion calculation checks an algebraic subconstruction; proving the general trace theorem also requires geometric vanishing and the group-ring path construction. Blocked use: ['Treating finite cocycle tests as verification of the Bass trace theorem for arbitrary discrete groups.']
- **L15** (unknown): ABSENT: a proved mapping from the trace theorem to fluid extension constructions or container isolation is not supplied. Blocked use: ['Using trace support as a certificate for fluid smoothness or operating-system isolation.']

<a id="pm-formal"></a>
### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 207 (`lean/docs/207.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [The Bass trace conjecture and the characteristic-zero Kaplansky idempotent conjecture](../../preprints/The-Bass-trace-conjecture-for-complex-group-rings-September-24-2026/The-Bass-trace-conjecture-for-complex-group-rings-September-24-2026.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The formalization proves the complex Bass trace conjecture for every group: the Hattori–Stallings trace of each virtual class of finitely generated projective right $\mathbb C[G]$-modules vanishes on conjugacy classes of infinite-order elements. No finiteness, countability, or geometric hypothesis on $G$ is imposed.
>
> For torsion-free $G$, it identifies the trace on $K_0(\mathbb C[G])$ with the integral augmentation rank and proves the characteristic-zero Kaplansky idempotent conjecture: for every commutative unital domain $R$ of characteristic zero, an idempotent in $R[G]$ is $0$ or $1$.

```lean
import Mathlib

namespace OAI

/-! Traces and augmentation rank on the idempotent presentation of algebraic K₀. -/

namespace BassTrace

open scoped Classical

noncomputable def classCoefficient {G : Type*} [Group G]
    (g : G) (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) : ℂ := by
  classical
  exact a.coeff.sum fun h z => if IsConj h g then z else 0

variable {G : Type*} [Group G]

@[simp] theorem classCoefficient_zero (g : G) :
    classCoefficient g 0 = 0 := by
  simp [classCoefficient]

@[simp] theorem classCoefficient_single (g h : G) (z : ℂ) :
    classCoefficient g (MonoidAlgebra.single h z) = if IsConj h g then z else 0 := by
  classical
  simp [classCoefficient, Finsupp.sum_single_index]

@[simp] theorem classCoefficient_add (g : G) (a b : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) :
    classCoefficient g (a + b) = classCoefficient g a + classCoefficient g b := by
  classical
  apply Finsupp.sum_add_index
  · intro h _
    simp
  · intro h _ z w
    split_ifs <;> simp

@[simp] theorem classCoefficient_smul (g : G) (z : ℂ) (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) :
    classCoefficient g (z • a) = z * classCoefficient g a := by
  classical
  unfold classCoefficient
  rw [MonoidAlgebra.coeff_smul, Finsupp.sum_smul_index (fun _ => by simp)]
  rw [Finsupp.sum, Finsupp.sum, Finset.mul_sum]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro h _
  split_ifs <;> simp

@[simp] theorem classCoefficient_sum {ι : Type*} (g : G) (s : Finset ι)
    (f : ι → MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) :
    classCoefficient g (∑ i ∈ s, f i) = ∑ i ∈ s, classCoefficient g (f i) := by
  classical
  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
  | empty => simp
  | @insert a s ha ih => simp [ha, ih]

theorem classCoefficient_mul_comm (g : G) (a b : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) :
    classCoefficient g (a * b) = classCoefficient g (b * a) := by
  induction a using MonoidAlgebra.induction_on with
  | of h =>
      induction b using MonoidAlgebra.induction_on with
      | of k =>
          have hconj : IsConj (h * k) (k * h) := by
            apply isConj_iff.mpr
            exact ⟨k, by simp [mul_assoc]⟩
          have hiff : IsConj (h * k) g ↔ IsConj (k * h) g :=
            ⟨hconj.symm.trans, hconj.trans⟩
          change classCoefficient g (MonoidAlgebra.single h 1 * MonoidAlgebra.single k 1) =
            classCoefficient g (MonoidAlgebra.single k 1 * MonoidAlgebra.single h 1)
          simp only [MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single, classCoefficient_single, hiff]
      | add b c hb hc => simp only [mul_add, add_mul, classCoefficient_add, hb, hc]
      | smul z b hb => simp only [mul_smul_comm, smul_mul_assoc, classCoefficient_smul, hb]
  | add a c ha hc => simp only [add_mul, mul_add, classCoefficient_add, ha, hc]
  | smul z a ha => simp only [mul_smul_comm, smul_mul_assoc, classCoefficient_smul, ha]

theorem classCoefficient_matrix_mul_comm {ι κ : Type*} [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ]
    (g : G) (a : Matrix ι κ (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G))
    (b : Matrix κ ι (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) :
    classCoefficient g (Matrix.trace (a * b)) =
      classCoefficient g (Matrix.trace (b * a)) := by
  classical
  simp only [Matrix.trace, Matrix.diag, Matrix.mul_apply, classCoefficient_sum]
  rw [Finset.sum_comm]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro j _
  exact classCoefficient_mul_comm g _ _

end BassTrace

noncomputable section

namespace BassTrace

open scoped Classical

namespace K0

open scoped Classical BigOperators

section

structure Idempotent (R : Type*) [Ring R] where
  size : ℕ
  matrix : Matrix (Fin size) (Fin size) R
  idem : matrix*matrix=matrix

variable {R : Type*} [Ring R]

def directSum (e f : Idempotent R) : Idempotent R where
  size := e.size+f.size
  matrix := Matrix.reindexRingEquiv R finSumFinEquiv (Matrix.fromBlocks e.matrix 0 0 f.matrix)
  idem := by
    rw [←map_mul,Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_zero,Matrix.zero_mul,add_zero,zero_add,e.idem,f.idem]

def relationGenerators (R : Type*) [Ring R] : Set (FreeAbelianGroup (Idempotent R)) :=
  {x | (∃e f : Idempotent R,∃a : Matrix (Fin e.size) (Fin f.size) R,
    ∃b : Matrix (Fin f.size) (Fin e.size) R,
    a*b=e.matrix ∧ b*a=f.matrix ∧ x=FreeAbelianGroup.of e-FreeAbelianGroup.of f) ∨
    (∃e f : Idempotent R,x=FreeAbelianGroup.of (directSum e f)-FreeAbelianGroup.of e-FreeAbelianGroup.of f)}

def relations (R : Type*) [Ring R] : AddSubgroup (FreeAbelianGroup (Idempotent R)) :=
  AddSubgroup.closure (relationGenerators R)

instance relations_normal (R : Type*) [Ring R] : (relations R).Normal := inferInstance

abbrev Group (R : Type*) [Ring R] := FreeAbelianGroup (Idempotent R) ⧸ relations R

variable {G : Type*} [_root_.Group G]

instance groupRingRelations_normal : (relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)).Normal := inferInstance

def rawCoefficient (g : G) : FreeAbelianGroup (Idempotent (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) →+ ℂ :=
  FreeAbelianGroup.lift (fun e=>classCoefficient g (Matrix.trace e.matrix))

end

end K0

end BassTrace

end

open Set MeasureTheory
open Finset MeasureTheory intervalIntegral
noncomputable section

namespace BassTrace.K0

section

open scoped Classical BigOperators

variable {R : Type*} [Ring R]

lemma trace_reindex {α β : Type*} [Fintype α] [Fintype β]
    (j : α≃β) (a : Matrix α α R) :
    Matrix.trace (Matrix.reindexRingEquiv R j a)=Matrix.trace a := by
  change (∑i, a (j.symm i) (j.symm i))=∑i,a i i
  exact Equiv.sum_comp j.symm (fun i=>a i i)

lemma trace_directSum (e f : Idempotent R) :
    Matrix.trace (directSum e f).matrix=Matrix.trace e.matrix+Matrix.trace f.matrix := by
  rw [directSum,trace_reindex]
  simp only [Matrix.trace,Matrix.diag,Fintype.sum_sum_type,Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₁₁,Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₂]

variable {G : Type*} [_root_.Group G]

lemma relations_in_kernel (g : G) : relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)≤(rawCoefficient g).ker := by
  apply (AddSubgroup.closure_le _).mpr
  intro x hx
  rcases hx with ⟨e,f,a,b,hab,hba,rfl⟩|⟨e,f,rfl⟩
  · change rawCoefficient g (FreeAbelianGroup.of e-FreeAbelianGroup.of f)=0
    simp only [map_sub,rawCoefficient,FreeAbelianGroup.lift_apply_of]
    apply sub_eq_zero.mpr
    rw [←hab,←hba]
    exact classCoefficient_matrix_mul_comm g a b
  · change rawCoefficient g (FreeAbelianGroup.of (directSum e f)-FreeAbelianGroup.of e-FreeAbelianGroup.of f)=0
    simp only [map_sub,rawCoefficient,FreeAbelianGroup.lift_apply_of,trace_directSum,classCoefficient_add]
    abel

def hsCoefficient (g : G) : Group (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) →+ ℂ :=
  QuotientAddGroup.lift (relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) (rawCoefficient g) (relations_in_kernel g)

end

end BassTrace.K0

end

open scoped Classical BigOperators
noncomputable section
namespace TorsionFreeBass

/-- A group is torsion-free when its only finite-order element is the identity.
This is the usual group-theoretic condition, not the stronger unique-roots condition. -/
def TorsionFree (G : Type*) [Group G] : Prop := ∀ g : G, IsOfFinOrder g → g = 1

variable {G : Type*} [Group G]

/-- The sum of coefficients, or augmentation, of the complex group algebra. -/
def augmentation : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G →+* ℂ :=
  (MonoidAlgebra.lift ℂ ℂ G (1 : G →* ℂ)).toRingHom

/-- The canonical trace is the coefficient of the identity. -/
def canonicalTrace (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) : ℂ := a.coeff 1

/-- The matrix trace obtained by applying the canonical trace to each diagonal entry. -/
def matrixTrace {q : ℕ} (e : Matrix (Fin q) (Fin q) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) : ℂ :=
  ∑ i, canonicalTrace (e i i)

/-- Entrywise augmentation. -/
def augmentedMatrix {m n : ℕ} (e : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin n) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) :
    Matrix (Fin m) (Fin n) ℂ := e.map augmentation

lemma augmentedMatrix_mul {m n p : ℕ}
    (a : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin n) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G))
    (b : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin p) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) :
    augmentedMatrix (a * b) = augmentedMatrix a * augmentedMatrix b := by
  exact Matrix.map_mul

lemma augmentedMatrix_trace {q : ℕ}
    (e : Matrix (Fin q) (Fin q) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) :
    Matrix.trace (augmentedMatrix e) = augmentation (Matrix.trace e) := by
  simp [augmentedMatrix, Matrix.trace, Matrix.diag]

lemma idempotent_matrix_trace_eq_rank {q : ℕ} (a : Matrix (Fin q) (Fin q) ℂ)
    (ha : a * a = a) : Matrix.trace a = (a.rank : ℂ) := by
  have h : IsIdempotentElem a.toLin' := by
    change a.toLin'.comp a.toLin' = a.toLin'
    rw [← Matrix.toLin'_mul, ha]
  rw [← Matrix.trace_toLin'_eq, (LinearMap.IsIdempotentElem.isProj_range _ h).trace]
  rfl

open BassTrace.K0 in
/-- The augmentation rank on the free abelian group of idempotents, before imposing
isomorphism and direct-sum relations. -/
def rawAugmentationRank : FreeAbelianGroup (Idempotent (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) →+ ℤ :=
  FreeAbelianGroup.lift (fun e => ((augmentedMatrix e.matrix).rank : ℤ))

lemma rawAugmentationRank_of (e : BassTrace.K0.Idempotent (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G)) :
    ((rawAugmentationRank (FreeAbelianGroup.of e) : ℤ) : ℂ) =
      augmentation (Matrix.trace e.matrix) := by
  change (((augmentedMatrix e.matrix).rank : ℤ) : ℂ) = _
  rw [Int.cast_natCast, ← idempotent_matrix_trace_eq_rank]
  · exact augmentedMatrix_trace _
  · rw [← augmentedMatrix_mul, e.idem]

lemma augmentationRank_relations : BassTrace.K0.relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) ≤
    (rawAugmentationRank (G := G)).ker := by
  apply (AddSubgroup.closure_le _).mpr
  intro x hx
  rcases hx with ⟨e,f,a,b,hab,hba,rfl⟩ | ⟨e,f,rfl⟩
  · change rawAugmentationRank (FreeAbelianGroup.of e - FreeAbelianGroup.of f) = 0
    apply Int.cast_injective (α := ℂ)
    rw [map_sub, Int.cast_sub, rawAugmentationRank_of, rawAugmentationRank_of, Int.cast_zero]
    apply sub_eq_zero.mpr
    rw [← augmentedMatrix_trace, ← augmentedMatrix_trace, ← hab, ← hba,
      augmentedMatrix_mul, augmentedMatrix_mul]
    exact Matrix.trace_mul_comm _ _
  · change rawAugmentationRank (FreeAbelianGroup.of (BassTrace.K0.directSum e f) -
      FreeAbelianGroup.of e - FreeAbelianGroup.of f) = 0
    apply Int.cast_injective (α := ℂ)
    simp only [map_sub, Int.cast_sub, rawAugmentationRank_of, Int.cast_zero,
      BassTrace.K0.trace_directSum, map_add]
    abel

/-- The integer-valued augmentation rank on the usual idempotent presentation of
algebraic K₀: on a generator it is the complex dimension of the augmented image. -/
def augmentationRank : BassTrace.K0.Group (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) →+ ℤ :=
  QuotientAddGroup.lift (BassTrace.K0.relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G))
    rawAugmentationRank augmentationRank_relations

open BassTrace.K0 in
lemma traceK0_relations : relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) ≤ (rawCoefficient (1 : G)).ker := by
  apply (AddSubgroup.closure_le _).mpr
  intro element membership
  rcases membership with
    ⟨idemLeft, idemRight, forward, backward, forward_backward, backward_forward, rfl⟩ |
    ⟨idemLeft, idemRight, rfl⟩
  · change rawCoefficient 1 (FreeAbelianGroup.of idemLeft - FreeAbelianGroup.of idemRight) = 0
    rw [map_sub]
    apply sub_eq_zero.mpr
    simp only [rawCoefficient, FreeAbelianGroup.lift_apply_of]
    rw [← forward_backward, ← backward_forward]
    exact BassTrace.classCoefficient_matrix_mul_comm 1 forward backward
  · change rawCoefficient 1 (FreeAbelianGroup.of (directSum idemLeft idemRight) -
      FreeAbelianGroup.of idemLeft - FreeAbelianGroup.of idemRight) = 0
    rw [map_sub, map_sub]
    simp only [rawCoefficient, FreeAbelianGroup.lift_apply_of]
    rw [trace_directSum, BassTrace.classCoefficient_add, add_sub_cancel_left, sub_self]

/-- Coefficient at the identity class of the Hattori--Stallings trace on K₀. -/
def traceK0 : BassTrace.K0.Group (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) →+ ℂ :=
  QuotientAddGroup.lift (BassTrace.K0.relations (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G))
    (BassTrace.K0.rawCoefficient 1) traceK0_relations

/-- Trace/rank, equality and range on algebraic K₀, and characteristic-zero
scalar idempotents for torsion-free groups. -/
theorem torsion_free_corollary (G : Type*) [Group G] (hG : TorsionFree G) :
    ((∀ (q : ℕ), 1 ≤ q →
      ∀ e : Matrix (Fin q) (Fin q) (MonoidAlgebra ℂ G), e ^ 2 = e →
        matrixTrace e = ((augmentedMatrix e).rank : ℂ)) ∧
      traceK0 (G := G) = (Int.castAddHom ℂ).comp augmentationRank ∧
      Set.range (traceK0 (G := G)) = Set.range (fun z : ℤ => (z : ℂ))) ∧
    (∀ (R : Type*) [CommRing R] [IsDomain R] [CharZero R]
      (e : MonoidAlgebra R G), e ^ 2 = e → e = 0 ∨ e = 1) := by
  sorry

end TorsionFreeBass
end

end OAI
```

```json
  "scope": {
    "doc": "lean/docs/207.md",
    "family": "207",
    "text": "The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):\n\n- [The Bass trace conjecture and the characteristic-zero Kaplansky idempotent conjecture](../../preprints/The-Bass-trace-conjecture-for-complex-group-rings-September-24-2026/The-Bass-trace-conjecture-for-complex-group-rings-September-24-2026.pdf)\n\n## Scope\n\nThe formalization proves the complex Bass trace conjecture for every group: the Hattori–Stallings trace of each virtual class of finitely generated projective right $\\mathbb C[G]$-modules vanishes on conjugacy classes of infinite-order elements. No finiteness, countability, or geometric hypothesis on $G$ is imposed.\n\nFor torsion-free $G$, it identifies the trace on $K_0(\\mathbb C[G])$ with the integral augmentation rank and proves the characteristic-zero Kaplansky idempotent conjecture: for every commutative unital domain $R$ of characteristic zero, an idempotent in $R[G]$ is $0$ or $1$."
  },
  "status": "present",
  "toolchain": "leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1"
```

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


We prove the complex group-ring Bass trace conjecture for every discrete group: the Hattori–Stallings trace of a finitely generated projective module over its complex group ring is supported on conjugacy classes of finite-order elements. As a consequence, for every torsion-free group <i>G</i> and every commutative unital domain <i>R</i> of characteristic zero, the only idempotents in $`RG`$ are 0 and 1. This proves Kaplansky's idempotent conjecture in characteristic zero.

<a id="pm-h-1"></a>
